# Mutual Recognition Act 1992 - Section 32 - Ministerial Declaration (9/02/2007)

**MUTUAL RECOGNITION ACT 1992 – Section 32**

**Ministerial Declaration**

To give effect to the express wish of the Council of Australian Governments (COAG) for full and effective mutual recognition of registered occupations, we the ministers today responsible jointly declare that:

The occupation which may be carried on only by a person granted registration (in the form of a licence, certificate, registration, or other instrument) in the first jurisdiction described in Column A in each of the attached Schedules is equivalent to the occupation which may be carried on only by a person granted registration in a second jurisdiction as described in the other columns of the same row in the relevant Schedule, subject to any conditions, limitations or restrictions as indicated.

The Honourable Morris Iemma MP ) (signed)

**Premier of New South Wales** ) Morris Iemma

The Honourable Steve Bracks MP ) (signed)

**Premier of Victoria** ) Steve Bracks

The Honourable Peter Beattie MP ) (signed)

**Premier of Queensland** ) Peter Beattie

The Honourable Alan Carpenter MLA ) (signed)

**Premier of Western Australia** ) Alan Carpenter

The Honourable Mike Rann MP ) (signed)

**Premier of South Australia** ) Mike Rann

The Honourable Paul Lennon MHA ) (signed)

**Premier of Tasmania** ) Paul Lennon

Katy Gallagher MLA ) (signed)

**Acting Chief Minister of the**\
**Australian Capital Territory** ) Katy Gallagher

The Honourable Clare Martin ML ) (signed)

**Chief Minister of the Northern Territory of**\
**Australia** ) Clare Martin

Dated this …ninth………..day of …February………. 2007

**Notes:**

The table at each of the attached Schedules depicts the registration outcome for any person already licensed in an occupation in a particular jurisdiction (column A of each row) should they seek to be licensed in an occupation in a second jurisdiction (potentially in any other column in the same row). Registration shall be granted by the second jurisdiction in the terms specified provided the applicant is already registered in the equivalent occupation in the first jurisdiction.

If an applicant for mutual recognition holds a licence shown in Column A of a Schedule, but has additional condition/s applying to that licence not shown in the Schedule, then the relevant registration authority in the second jurisdiction shall recognise the primary licence in Column A and may, in issuing a licence, apply those additional conditions to directly replicate the conditions applying to the first jurisdiction licence.

If a licence is not included in the declaration, or if the licence outcome depicted on a Schedule is the statement ‘No equivalent declared’, the decision on licence recognition will be made by the relevant registration authority in accordance with the other provisions of the *Mutual Recognition Act 1992*.

Licences covered by this declaration are licences that are issued to natural persons, not licences issued to companies.

This declaration and any subsequent declarations will be reviewed for accuracy each year. Declarations and any revised declarations will be published on the COAG mutual recognition website and the websites of the relevant licensing authorities.

<table>
<tr>
<td colspan="2"><b>Codes for conditions (restrictions, inclusions and exclusions) applying to Builders’ Licences</b></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Basements</td>
<td>Basement construction</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>BCA</td>
<td>Specified classes of the Building Code of Australia. BCA means the Classification of Buildings and Structures under Part A3 of the Building Code of Australia as amended published by the Australian Building Codes Board.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Cellars</td>
<td>Cellar construction</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Com</td>
<td>Commercial building construction</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>CT-A</td>
<td>Type A construction (BCA)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>CT-B</td>
<td>Type B construction (BCA)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>CT-C</td>
<td>Type C construction (BCA)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Dem</td>
<td>Demolition work</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Des</td>
<td>Design work for construction</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Dom</td>
<td>Domestic building construction</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Exca</td>
<td>Excavation work (if limited in height, describe the height)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>FA (specify area)</td>
<td>Maximum specific floor area(s)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>H≤(specify height)</td>
<td>Indicates buildings of less than or equal to the indicated number of storeys or height</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>H=(specify height)</td>
<td>Indicates building of precisely the indicated number of storeys or height</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>H≥(specify height)</td>
<td>Indicates buildings of greater than or equal to the indicated number of storeys or height</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>NSF</td>
<td>Non-Structural Fitout</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>SF</td>
<td>Structural Fitout</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>SP</td>
<td>Swimming Pool Construction</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>SS</td>
<td>Steel framing and/or steel roof structures</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>SW</td>
<td>Specified work including prefabricated buildings, asbestos handling, removal and/or re-siting of buildings, structural landscaping fencing, glazing, etc</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>U</td>
<td>Unrestricted. Denotes where it is intended that there be no limit such as in floor area or heights</td>
</tr>
</table>

**Codes for statutes under which Plumbing Occupations Licences are issued**

1\. Licences in this column issued under the Home Building Act 1989 (NSW)

2\. Licences in this column issued under the Building Act 1993 (VIC)

3\. Plumbers and Drainers Licences in this column issued under the Plumbing and Drainage Act 2002 (QLD)\
Gas Licences in this column issued under the Petroleum and Gas (Production & Safety) Act 2004 (QLD)\
Trade Contractor Licence in this column issued under the Queensland Building Services Authority Act 1991 (QLD)

4\. Plumbers Licences in this column issued under the Water Services Licensing Act 1995 (WA)\
Gas Licences in this column issued under the Gas Standards Act 1972 (WA)

5\. Licences in this column issued the Plumbers, Gas-fitters and Electricians Act 1995 (SA)

6\. Licences in this column issued under the Plumbers and Gas-fitters Registration Act 1951 (TAS)

7\. Licences in this column issued under the Construction Occupations (Licensing) Act 2004 (ACT)\
Gas Workers Accreditation Type A and Type B in this column issued under the Gas Safety Act 2000 (ACT)

8\. Plumbers and Drainers Licences in this column issued under the Plumbers and Drainers Act 2003 (NT)\
Gas-fitter Licences in this column issued under the Dangerous Goods Act 2004 (NT)

**Codes for statutes under which Plumbing Occupations licences are issued**

1\. Licences in this column issued under the Home Building Act 1989 (NSW)

2\. Licences in this column issued under the Building Act 1993 (VIC)

3\. Plumbers and Drainers Licences in this column issued under the Plumbing and Drainage Act 2002 (QLD)\
Gas Licences in this column issued under the Petroleum and Gas (Production & Safety) Act 2004 (QLD)\
Trade Contractor Licence in this column issued under the Queensland Building Services Authority Act 1991 (QLD)

4\. Plumbers Licences in this column issued under the Water Services Licensing Act 1995 (WA)\
Gas Licences in this column issues under the Gas Standards Act 1972 (WA)

5\. Licences in this column issued the Plumbers, Gas-fitters and Electricians Act 1995 (SA)

6\. Licences in this column issued under the Plumbers and Gas-fitters Registration Act 1951 (TAS)

7\. Licences in this column issued under the Construction Occupations (Licensing) Act 2004 (ACT)\
Gas Workers Accreditation Type A and Type B in this column issued under the Gas Safety Act 2000 (ACT)

8\. Plumbers and Drainers Licences in this column issued under the Plumbers and Drainers Act 2003 (NT)\
Gas-fitter Licences in this column issued under the Dangerous Goods Act 2004 (NT)

**Codes for Statutes under which Automotive gas installer licences are issued:**

Licences issued in this column under the Motor Vehicle Repairs Act 1980(NSW)

Licences issued in this column under the Dangerous Goods Act 1998 (VIC)

Licences issued in this column under the Gas Standards Act 1972 (WA)

Licences issued in this column under the Dangerous Substances Act 1979 (SA)

Licences issued in this column under the Dangerous Goods Act 1998 (TAS)

Licences issued in this column under the Construction Occupations (Licensing) Act 2004 (ACT)

Licences issued in this column under the Dangerous Goods Act 1998 (NT)

**Codes for Scope of Work for Automotive gas installers:**

**Licences shown in the table above may include some or all of these scopes of work.**

| IN | Installation |
|---|---|
| MS | Maintenance/Servicing |
| RE | Repairing |
| AM | Altering/Modifying |
| TE | Testing |
| CO | Commissioning |
| CE | Certifying compliance with relevant Australian Standards and jurisdictional regulatory requirements |
| LPG | Liquefied Petroleum Gas |
| LNG | Liquefied Natural Gas |
| CNG | Compressed Natural Gas |

**Codes for statutes under which Air-conditioning and Refrigeration Mechanics licences are issued**

1\. Licences in this column issued under the Home Building Act 1989 Section 27 (NSW)

2\. Licences in this column issued under the Building Act 1993 (VIC)

3\. Licences in this column issued under the Queensland Building Services Authority Act 1991(QLD)\
NB *Electrical work of a like-for-like nature and disconnect / reconnect incidental to the Refrigeration Mechanic's trade will require the possession of a Restricted Electrical Work Licence, issued under the Electrical Safety Act 2002.*

4\. Licences in this column issued under the Building Work Contractors Act 1995 (SA)

5\. Licences in this column issued under the Plumbers and Gas-fitters Registration Act 1951(TAS)

<table>
<tr>
<td colspan="2"><b>Codes for Scopes of Work for Air-conditioning and Refrigeration Mechanics</b></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>ABW-A</td>
<td>Building work related to the installation, replacement, repair, alteration, maintenance, testing and commissioning of mechanical heating and cooling and ventilation systems in or of buildings.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>ABW-B</td>
<td>Building work related to the installation of ducting associated with mechanical heating and cooling and ventilation systems</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>AMSP-A</td>
<td>Construction, installation, replacement, repair, alteration, maintenance, testing and commissioning of mechanical heating, cooling and ventilation systems (excluding ducting) in or of buildings, (does not mean Gasfitting or Water plumbing).</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>AMSP-B</td>
<td>Construction, installation, replacement, repair, alteration, maintenance, testing and commissioning of mechanical heating, cooling and ventilation systems (excluding ducting) in or of a structure (that isn’t a building), (does not mean Gasfitting or Water plumbing).</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>AMSP-C</td>
<td>Construction, installation, replacement, repair, alteration, maintenance, testing and commissioning of mechanical heating, cooling and ventilation systems (excluding ducting) in a vessel (does not mean Gasfitting or Water plumbing).</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>AMSP-D</td>
<td>Construction, installation, replacement, repair, alteration, maintenance, testing and commissioning of mechanical heating, cooling and ventilation systems (excluding ducting) in or of a container (does not mean Gasfitting or Water plumbing).</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>AMSP-E</td>
<td>Construction, installation, replacement, repair, alteration, maintenance, testing and commissioning of mechanical heating, cooling and ventilation systems (excluding ducting) in or of a railway vehicle (does not mean Gasfitting or Water plumbing).</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>AMSP-F</td>
<td>Replacement, repair, alteration, maintenance, testing and commissioning of ducting associated with mechanical heating and cooling and ventilation systems in or of buildings.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>AMSP-G</td>
<td>Replacement, repair, alteration, maintenance, testing and commissioning of ducting associated with mechanical heating and cooling and ventilation systems in or of a vessel.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>ADW-A</td>
<td>Design of mechanical heating and cooling and ventilation systems in or of buildings subject to the following conditions (but does not mean Gasfitting or Water plumbing);</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>ADW-B</td>
<td>Restricted to systems of no greater than 18 Kilowatts capacity.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>ADW-C</td>
<td>Restricted to a class of building as defined under the Building Code of Australia.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>ADW-D</td>
<td>Restricted to a system designed to service a floor area of less than 2000 square metres.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>ADW-E</td>
<td>Restricted to a building height of less than 3 storeys.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>AEW</td>
<td>Disconnection and reconnection of domestic or commercial air-conditioning and refrigeration equipment or components and includes faultfinding of equipment to determine component failure. It includes installation work (excluding the installation of fixed wiring) and plug and cord faultfinding.<br><b>Note:</b> South Australia and Western Australia allows licensees for this class to install factory supplied fixed cable between split air-conditioning systems up to 4 Kilowatts.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>AC-S</td>
<td>Supervision only of the performance of any airconditioning work.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>RBW-A</td>
<td>Building work related to the installation, replacement, repair, alteration, maintenance, testing and commissioning of refrigeration equipment, including coolrooms, in buildings.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>RBW-B</td>
<td>Building work related to the installation of ducting associated with refrigeration systems</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>RMSP-A</td>
<td>Construction, installation, replacement, repair, alteration, maintenance, testing and commissioning of refrigeration equipment which is associated with mechanical heating and cooling and ventilation systems located in or of a building.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>RMSP-B</td>
<td>Construction, installation, replacement, repair, alteration, maintenance, testing and commissioning of refrigeration equipment which is associated with mechanical heating and cooling and ventilation systems located in or of a structure.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>RMSP-C</td>
<td>Construction, installation, replacement, repair, alteration, maintenance, testing and commissioning of refrigeration equipment which is associated with mechanical heating and cooling and ventilation systems located in or of a vessel.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>RMSP-D</td>
<td>Construction, installation, replacement, repair, alteration, maintenance, testing and commissioning of refrigeration equipment which is associated with mechanical heating and cooling and ventilation systems located in or of a container.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>RMSP-E</td>
<td>Construction, installation, replacement, repair, alteration, maintenance, testing and commissioning of refrigeration equipment which is associated with mechanical heating and cooling and ventilation systems located in or of a vehicle.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>RDW</td>
<td>Design of refrigeration equipment systems in buildings.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>REW</td>
<td>Disconnection and reconnection of domestic or commercial air-conditioning and refrigeration equipment or components and includes faultfinding of equipment to determine component failure. It includes installation work (excluding the installation of fixed wiring) and plug and cord faultfinding. <b>Note:</b>·South Australia and Western Australia allow licensees for this class to install factory supplied fixed cable between split air-conditioning systems up to 4 Kilowatts.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>R-S</td>
<td>Supervision only of the performance of any refrigeration work</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>CW</td>
<td>Contracting with clients for the performance of air-conditioning and/or refrigeration work or a class of that work, and/or certifying air-conditioning and/or refrigeration work or a class of that work.</td>
</tr>
</table>

**Codes for statutes under which Electrician licences are issued**

1\. Licences in this column issued under the *Home Building Act 1989* (NSW)

2\. Licences in this column issued under the *Electricity Safety Act 1998* (VIC)

3\. Licences in this column issued under the *Electrical Safety Act 2002* (QLD)

4\. Licences in this column issued under the *Electricity Act 1945* (WA)

5\. Licences in this column issued under the *Plumbers Gasfitters and Electricians Act 1995* (SA)

6\. Licences in this column issued under the *Electricity Industry Safety and Administration Act 1997* (TAS)

7\. Licences in this column issued under the *Construction Occupations (Licensing) Act 2004* (ACT)

8\. Licences in this column issued under the *Electrical Workers and Contractors Act 2002* (NT)

**Agreed scope of work for all electrician licences in the above table**

“Licensed electrician” means a person who is licensed to undertake unsupervised electrical work in a jurisdiction.

“Electrical work” means, selection, assembling, constructing, installing, testing, fault-finding, commissioning, maintaining, repairing, altering, removing, or replacing of electrical equipment and/or electrical installations. It may include the supervision or inspection of electrical work.

"Electrical equipment" means any appliance, article, accessory, wire, fitting, cable, conduit or apparatus that generates, uses, conveys or controls (or that is intended to, or has been used to, generate, use, convey or control) electricity operating above extra low voltage.

“Extra low voltage” means as defined in AS/NZS 3000"Electrical installation" is as defined in AS/NZS 3000 (commonly known as the “wiring rules”).

**Schedule 13: Mutual recognition matrix – Restricted electrical licences**

**Note: Prior to the issue of an equivalent restricted electrical licence in this table through the mutual recognition process, the need for the issue of the equivalent licence must be established by the jurisdictional regulator based on the demonstrated requirement for the performance of this work in the specific job being undertaken.**

**In some instances a Restricted Electrical Licence (REL) will only be given when the applicant holds a relevant licence in their primary trade, for example a plumbing licence or a gas-fitting licence.**

**Codes for statutes under which Restricted Electrical Licences are issued:**

Licences in this column are issued under the Home Building Act 1989 (NSW)

Licences in this column are issued under the Electrical Safety Act 1998 (VIC)

Licences in this column are issued under the Electrical Safety Act 2002 (QLD)

Licences in this column are issued under the Electrical Act 1945 (WA)

Licences in this column are issued under the Plumbers Gasfitter and Electricians Act 1995 (SA)

Licences in this column are issued under the Electricity Industry Safety and Administration Act 1997 (TAS)

Licences in this column are issued under the Construction Occupations (Licensing) Act 2004 (ACT)

Licences in this column are issued under the Electrical Workers and Contractors Act 2002 (NT)
